\widetilde{\varphi}_{i,i^{\prime}}^{j}(\mu)=(h_{i^{\prime},i}^{j})^{-n}\mu^{\prime}
,

Proof.

Since
\mathcal{I}_{S}
is locally finitely PD-generated,
the PD ideals
I_{K_{i}}\vcentcolon=\mathcal{I}_{S}(S_{i})\subseteq K_{i}

with PD structure
\gamma_{K_{i}}\vcentcolon=\gamma_{S}(S_{i})

are finitely PD-generated,
and since
X
is locally of finite presentation over
S_{0}
,
each
R_{i}
is a finitely presented
K_{i}/I_{K_{i}}
-algebra.
So
(X_{i}/S_{i})_{\mathrm{Cris}_{\mathrm{fp}}}
classifies

\mathbb{T}_{K_{i},R_{i}}=K_{i}\text{-}\mathrm{Alg}\text{-}R_{i}\text{-}\mathrm{Quot}+\mathrm{PD}_{\gamma_{K_{i}}}+(\mathrm{nil})+(\mathrm{loc})\hbox to0.0pt{.\hss}

By Lemma
5.8.1
,
these toposes

\mathcal{E}_{i}\vcentcolon=((X/S)_{\mathrm{Cris}_{\mathrm{fp}}})_{o(U_{X_{i}})}\simeq(X_{i}/S_{i})_{\mathrm{Cris}_{\mathrm{fp}}}

form an open cover of
(X/S)_{\mathrm{Cris}_{\mathrm{fp}}}
,
and the universal models are extensions of
the restrictions of the short exact sequence

\mathcal{J}\hookrightarrow\mathcal{O}\twoheadrightarrow\mathcal{O}^{\prime}

surrounding the structure sheaf.
So we can use the base theory
and base model

\displaystyle\mathbb{T}_{0}

\displaystyle\vcentcolon=\mathbb{Z}\text{-}\mathrm{Alg}\text{-}\mathbb{Z}\text{-}\mathrm{Quot}+\mathrm{PD}+(\mathrm{nil})+(\mathrm{loc})\hbox to0.0pt{,\hss}

\displaystyle M_{0}

\displaystyle\vcentcolon=(\mathcal{J}\hookrightarrow\mathcal{O}\twoheadrightarrow\mathcal{O}^{\prime})\hbox to0.0pt{,\hss}

where
\mathbb{T}_{0}
is of course equivalent to

\mathrm{Ring}+\mathrm{Ideal}+\mathrm{PD}+(\mathrm{nil})+(\mathrm{loc})
,
but we want to have the sort
B=A/I

in the base theory.
And we have presentations of the
\mathcal{E}_{i}

over
(\mathbb{T}_{0},M_{0})

with

\mathbb{E}_{i}\vcentcolon={K_{i}\text{-}\mathrm{AlgStr}}(A)+{R_{i}\text{-}\mathrm{AlgStr}}_{K_{i}}(B)+\gamma/\gamma_{K_{i}}\hbox to0.0pt{.\hss}

Closed geometric formulas for the intersections

\mathcal{E}_{i}\cap\mathcal{E}_{i^{\prime}}\subseteq\mathcal{E}_{i}

are given by

\phi_{i,i^{\prime}}\;\vcentcolon=\;\bigvee_{j\in J_{\{i,i^{\prime}\}}}\inv(c_{h_{i,i^{\prime}}^{j}})\hbox to0.0pt{.\hss}

The diagonal quotient extensions
\mathbb{Q}_{\{i,i^{\prime}\}}

have to make both the
K_{i^{\prime}}
-algebra structure on
A

and the
R_{i^{\prime}}
-algebra structure on
B

definable in terms of
\mathbb{T}_{0}+\mathbb{E}_{i}+\phi_{i,i^{\prime}}
.
Since the formula
\phi_{i,i^{\prime}}
also implies

\bigvee_{j\in J_{\{i,i^{\prime}\}}}\inv(c_{g_{i,i^{\prime}}^{j}})\hbox to0.0pt{,\hss}

we can proceed as in Theorem
3.7.6

for both
K_{i}
and
R_{i}
.
That is,
for every
\lambda\in K_{i}

and
\mu\in R_{i}
,
we write

\varphi_{i,i^{\prime}}^{j}(\lambda)=(g_{i^{\prime},i}^{j})^{-n}\lambda^{\prime}

for some
\lambda^{\prime}\in K_{i^{\prime}}
,
n\in\mathbb{N}
,
respectively

\widetilde{\varphi}_{i,i^{\prime}}^{j}(\mu)=(h_{i^{\prime},i}^{j})^{-n}\mu^{\prime}

for some
\mu^{\prime}\in R_{i^{\prime}}
,
n\in\mathbb{N}
.
Then
\mathcal{O}_{((X_{i}\cap X_{i^{\prime}})/(S_{i}\cap S_{i^{\prime}}))_{\mathrm{Cris}_{\mathrm{fp}}}}

satisfies

\displaystyle\mathbb{Q}_{i,i^{\prime}}\quad

\displaystyle\vcentcolon=\quad\Bigl\{\quad\inv(c_{g_{i^{\prime},i}^{j}})\;\vdash_{[]}\;(c_{g_{i^{\prime},i}^{j}})^{n}c_{\lambda}=c_{\lambda^{\prime}}\quad\mid\quad j\in J_{\{i,i^{\prime}\}},\lambda\in K_{i}\quad\Bigr\}\hbox to0.0pt{,\hss}

\displaystyle\widetilde{\mathbb{Q}}_{i,i^{\prime}}\quad

\displaystyle\vcentcolon=\quad\Bigl\{\quad\inv(c_{h_{i^{\prime},i}^{j}})\;\vdash_{[]}\;(c_{h_{i^{\prime},i}^{j}})^{n}c_{\mu}=c_{\mu^{\prime}}\quad\mid\quad j\in J_{\{i,i^{\prime}\}},\mu\in R_{i}\quad\Bigr\}\hbox to0.0pt{,\hss}

and

\mathbb{Q}_{\{i,i^{\prime}\}}\vcentcolon=\mathbb{Q}_{i,i^{\prime}}+\mathbb{Q}_{i^{\prime},i}+\widetilde{\mathbb{Q}}_{i,i^{\prime}}+\widetilde{\mathbb{Q}}_{i^{\prime},i}

is a diagonal quotient extension
of
\mathbb{E}_{i}+\phi_{i,i^{\prime}}
and
\mathbb{E}_{i^{\prime}}+\phi_{i^{\prime},i}

over
\mathbb{T}_{0}
.

Applying Corollary
3.6.1

to these data
and simplifying the axioms slightly
yields the theory
\mathbb{T}_{X/S}
in the statement.
∎
